K2 engineering evidence — 2026-10-31
No production decoder/owner changes. FIFO timing batches remain staged at the exact recorded hashes.
Governing documents: WSE-STD-VOICE-TIERS §§2.3/3, WSE-STD-070A-CCS-POLYPHASE-IDWPT, WSE-STD-001, WSE-STD-210.
Fixed: N=64, K=2, stored int16, 512 logical working bytes + 128 output bytes, original RNE and node saturation.
Schedule: first level 8+6, four middle levels 4*(8+10), root 8+5 = 99 compute clocks.
Standalone ready-input transaction 179 clocks; integrated ready-frame service 183; fallback remains 18.
Each working array is physically split into eight 8x32 banks. Address bank={xor(addr[5:2]),addr[1:0]}, row=addr[5:3].
Relative to the shared-register candidate, retime/range add exactly 40 FF: 32 input data, 6 input address, input pending, node write valid.
No extra service clock. Final input row7 writes while first compute group reads row0.
Range candidate replaces power-of-two comparisons by exact sign-extension detection; all 17-bit inputs x 16 headrooms proved.
Mapped tests: complete standalone decoder, headroom2, 312 frames / 19,968 exact samples per retime/range source, with stalls/resets.
Model library and both includes are hashed. This is functional netlist simulation, not device or routed timing simulation.
Control proof limits: 200-clock bounded proof permits arbitrary stalls/resets from reset; unbounded induction attempts did not close.
Compute cutpoint proof: 100 clocks from explicitly assumed post-load control state, exact first audio validity at 99; loading invariant is not proved by that cutpoint.
Retained diagnostics: over-strict initial overlap assertion, missing mapped-model include, shared mapped host timeout at 180 seconds,
inconclusive induction runs, missing cutpoint flag, inactive load-mutation coverage gap, and negative-control log-format mismatch.
Corrected runs preserve the original hardware latency, clock target, formats and thresholds.
Owner variant retains the same three state bits and same transition policy; all-state/all-input proof plus corrupt-discard-state control.
Owner uses the identical range decoder; its integration timings include the owner Boolean rewrite.
Production K2 baseline costs 247 compute / 331 integrated ready-service clocks, LUT2807/FF2593/CCU348/DPR176/DP6.
Candidate costs must be compared against that baseline; parallel decode exchanges BRAM use for distributed RAM, logic and registers.
These decoder costs do not establish the full 100-cycle T2 workload, global 133333-clock block, 40000-clock DMA service, memory packing or board fit.
Mode A storage and runtime mixed-tier execution remain open.

===== banked: source and inventories =====
/* Isolated K2 four-pair candidate. N64, two int16 slots and all numeric
 * reductions are unchanged. Each 64x32 working bank is split into eight
 * 8x32 banks; bank={addr[2]^addr[3]^addr[4]^addr[5],addr[1:0]}, row=addr[5:3].
 * This is an invertible physical layout, not a different transform.
 * Level0 boundary pairs use odd=detail+wide_even after the original update.
 * Expected compute: (8+6)+4*(8+10)+(8+5)=99 clocks; not yet qualification.
 */
module ccs_idwpt #(parameter K=2, HEADROOM_BITS=2)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,coeff_valid,
    output wire coeff_ready,
    input wire [31:0] coeff_data,
    output wire audio_valid,
    input wire audio_ready,
    output wire [63:0] audio_data,
    output wire audio_last
);
    localparam LOAD=3'd0, UPDATE=3'd1, UPDATE_DRAIN=3'd2, OUTPUT=3'd5;
    reg [2:0] state,level,pair_group;
    reg [5:0] load_leaf;
    reg source_bank;
    reg [3:0] output_index;
    reg [5:0] update_valid;
    reg [3:0] predict_valid;
    reg [2:0] update_group [0:5],predict_group [0:3];
    reg [3:0] right_tag [0:4];
    reg [3:0] left_tag0,left_boundary1,right_boundary1;
    reg [3:0] left_boundary,right_boundary;
    reg [31:0] previous_detail;
    reg [33:0] pending_even [0:3];
    reg [31:0] pending_detail [0:3];
    reg [3:0] pending_right;
    reg [2:0] pending_group;
    reg pending_valid;
    wire predict_issue=(level!=0 && level!=5) && pending_valid && (pending_right[3] || update_valid[4]);
    wire [33:0] wide_even [0:3];
    wire [31:0] wide_detail [0:3],clipped_even [0:3],clipped_odd [0:3],final_samples [0:3];
    wire [31:0] short_even [0:3],short_odd [0:3];
    wire reading=rst_n && state==UPDATE;
    wire writing=rst_n && ((level==0 && update_valid[5]) || (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3]));
    wire [2:0] write_group=(level==0) ? update_group[5] : predict_group[3];
    wire [31:0] a_read [0:7],b_read [0:7];
    wire [31:0] source0 [0:3],source1 [0:3];

    function [5:0] address;
        input [2:0] current_level;
        input [4:0] pair_number;
        input detail;
        begin
            case(current_level)
                0: address={pair_number,detail};
                1: address={pair_number[4:1],detail,pair_number[0]};
                2: address={pair_number[4:2],detail,pair_number[1:0]};
                3: address={pair_number[4:3],detail,pair_number[2:0]};
                4: address={pair_number[4],detail,pair_number[3:0]};
                default: address={detail,pair_number};
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [2:0] bank_id;
        input [5:0] location;
        begin bank_id={^location[5:2],location[1:0]}; end
    endfunction
    reg [2:0] smooth_row;
    always @* begin
        case(level)
            3: smooth_row={pair_group[2:1],1'b0};
            4: smooth_row={pair_group[2],1'b0,pair_group[1]};
            5: smooth_row={1'b0,pair_group[2:1]};
            default: smooth_row=pair_group;
        endcase
    end
    genvar bank;
    generate for(bank=0;bank<8;bank=bank+1) begin: storage
        reg [31:0] a [0:7],b [0:7];
        reg [31:0] ar,br;
        wire detail_row=bank[2] ^ pair_group[0] ^ (^smooth_row);
        reg [2:0] row;
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                3: row={smooth_row[2:1],detail_row};
                4: row={smooth_row[2],detail_row,smooth_row[0]};
                5: row={detail_row,smooth_row[1:0]};
                default: row=pair_group;
            endcase
        end
        wire load=rst_n && state==LOAD && coeff_valid && bank_id(load_leaf)==bank;
        wire [1:0] pair_select={bank[2] ^ (^write_group),bank[1]};
        wire [31:0] even_data=(level==0) ? short_even[pair_select] : clipped_even[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] odd_data=(level==0) ? short_odd[pair_select] : clipped_odd[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] data=bank[0] ? odd_data : even_data;
        always @(posedge clk) begin
            if(load || (writing && source_bank)) a[load ? load_leaf[5:3] : write_group] <= load ? coeff_data : data;
            if(writing && !source_bank) b[write_group] <= data;
            if(reading && !source_bank) ar <= a[row];
            if(reading && source_bank) br <= b[row];
        end
        assign a_read[bank]=ar;
        assign b_read[bank]=br;
    end endgenerate

    genvar pair;
    generate for(pair=0;pair<4;pair=pair+1) begin: operands
        wire [5:0] smooth=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b0);
        wire [5:0] detail=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b1);
        assign source0[pair]=source_bank ? b_read[bank_id(smooth)] : a_read[bank_id(smooth)];
        assign source1[pair]=source_bank ? b_read[bank_id(detail)] : a_read[bank_id(detail)];
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                0: begin left_boundary[pair]=1;right_boundary[pair]=1;end
                1: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair[0];right_boundary[pair]=pair[0];end
                2: begin left_boundary[pair]=(pair==0);right_boundary[pair]=(pair==3);end
                3: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair_group[0] && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=pair_group[0] && pair==3;end
                4: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group[1:0]==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group[1:0]) && pair==3;end
                default: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group) && pair==3;end
            endcase
        end
    end endgenerate
    function signed [15:0] quarter_rne;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        reg signed [16:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 2;
            if ((value[1:0] == 2'b11) || ((value[1:0] == 2'b10) && value[2]))
                quotient = quotient + 17'sd1;
            quarter_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [16:0] half_rne;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        reg signed [17:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 1;
            if (value[0] && value[1]) quotient = quotient + 18'sd1;
            half_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] clamp18;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        begin
            // An 18-bit value fits int16 exactly when bits 17:15 are a
            // sign extension. Overflow polarity is the original sign bit.
            if (value[17:15] == 3'b000 || value[17:15] == 3'b111)
                clamp18 = value[15:0];
            else clamp18 = value[17] ? 16'sh8000 : 16'sh7fff;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] restore_headroom;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        begin
            // For h>=0, SAT16(SAT16(x)*2^h) == SAT16(x*2^h).
            // Compare before scaling; both thresholds are exact for h<=15.
            // The in-range shift is wiring, with no intermediate truncation.
            if (value > (17'sd32767 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = 16'sd32767;
            else if (value < (-17'sd32768 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = -16'sd32768;
            else restore_headroom = value[15:0] << HEADROOM_BITS;
        end
    endfunction



    genvar q,lane;
    generate for(q=0;q<4;q=q+1) begin: pairs
        for(lane=0;lane<2;lane=lane+1) begin: arithmetic
            reg signed [15:0] smooth1,detail1,left_detail1,smooth2,smooth3,update3;
            reg signed [16:0] detail_sum2,even4,left_even1,right_even1,average3;
            reg signed [17:0] even_sum2,odd4,short_odd5;
            reg signed [15:0] odd_detail1,odd_detail2,odd_detail3,even2,even3,even_for_write4;
            reg signed [15:0] detail2,detail3,detail4,short_even5;
            wire signed [15:0] selected_left1=left_boundary1[q] ? detail1 : left_detail1;
            wire signed [16:0] selected_right1=right_boundary1[q] ? left_even1 : right_even1;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
                if(update_valid[0]) begin
                    smooth1<=source0[q][16*lane +:16];
                    detail1<=source1[q][16*lane +:16];
                    if(q==0) left_detail1<=previous_detail[16*lane +:16];
                    else left_detail1<=source1[q-1][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(update_valid[1]) begin
                    detail_sum2<=$signed({selected_left1[15],selected_left1})+$signed({detail1[15],detail1});
                    smooth2<=smooth1;detail2<=detail1;
                end
                if(update_valid[2]) begin
                    update3<=quarter_rne(detail_sum2);smooth3<=smooth2;detail3<=detail2;
                end
                if(update_valid[3]) begin
                    even4<=$signed({smooth3[15],smooth3})-$signed({update3[15],update3});detail4<=detail3;
                end
                if(update_valid[4] && level==0) begin
                    short_even5<=clamp18({even4[16],even4});
                    short_odd5<=$signed({{2{detail4[15]}},detail4})+$signed({even4[16],even4});
                end
                if(predict_issue) begin
                    left_even1<=pending_even[q][17*lane +:17];
                    if(q==3) right_even1<=wide_even[0][17*lane +:17];
                    else right_even1<=pending_even[q+1][17*lane +:17];
                    odd_detail1<=pending_detail[q][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(predict_valid[0]) begin
                    even_sum2<=$signed({left_even1[16],left_even1})+$signed({selected_right1[16],selected_right1});
                    even2<=clamp18({left_even1[16],left_even1});odd_detail2<=odd_detail1;
                end
                if(predict_valid[1]) begin
                    average3<=half_rne(even_sum2);odd_detail3<=odd_detail2;even3<=even2;
                end
                if(predict_valid[2]) begin
                    odd4<=$signed({{2{odd_detail3[15]}},odd_detail3})+$signed({average3[16],average3});
                    even_for_write4<=even3;
                end
            end
            assign wide_even[q][17*lane +:17]=even4;
            assign wide_detail[q][16*lane +:16]=detail4;
            assign final_samples[q][16*lane +:16]=restore_headroom(even4);
            assign clipped_even[q][16*lane +:16]=even_for_write4;
            assign clipped_odd[q][16*lane +:16]=clamp18(odd4);
            assign short_even[q][16*lane +:16]=short_even5;
            assign short_odd[q][16*lane +:16]=clamp18(short_odd5);
        end
        always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_even[q]<=wide_even[q];pending_detail[q]<=wide_detail[q];
        end
    end endgenerate

    reg [63:0] output_lo [0:7],output_hi [0:7];
    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if(rst_n && update_valid[0]) begin previous_detail<=source1[3];left_boundary1<=left_tag0;end
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_right<=right_tag[4];pending_group<=update_group[4];
        end
        if(rst_n && predict_issue) right_boundary1<=pending_right;
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level==5) begin
            output_lo[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[1],final_samples[0]};
            output_hi[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[3],final_samples[2]};
        end
    end
    assign coeff_ready=rst_n && state==LOAD;
    assign audio_valid=rst_n && state==OUTPUT;
    assign audio_last=audio_valid && output_index==15;
    assign audio_data=output_index[0] ? output_hi[output_index[3:1]] : output_lo[output_index[3:1]];
    integer stage;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            state<=LOAD;level<=0;pair_group<=0;load_leaf<=0;source_bank<=0;output_index<=0;
            update_valid<=0;predict_valid<=0;pending_valid<=0;
        end else begin
            update_valid<={update_valid[4:0],state==UPDATE};
            predict_valid<={predict_valid[2:0],predict_issue};
            update_group[0]<=pair_group;predict_group[0]<=pending_group;
            left_tag0<=left_boundary;right_tag[0]<=right_boundary;
            for(stage=1;stage<6;stage=stage+1) update_group[stage]<=update_group[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<5;stage=stage+1) right_tag[stage]<=right_tag[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<4;stage=stage+1) predict_group[stage]<=predict_group[stage-1];
            if(update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) pending_valid<=1;
            else if(predict_issue) pending_valid<=0;
            case(state)
                LOAD: if(coeff_valid) begin
                    if(load_leaf==63) begin load_leaf<=0;pair_group<=0;level<=0;source_bank<=0;state<=UPDATE;end
                    else load_leaf<=load_leaf+1'b1;
                end
                UPDATE: if(pair_group==7) state<=UPDATE_DRAIN;
                        else pair_group<=pair_group+1'b1;
                UPDATE_DRAIN: begin
                    if(level==5 && update_valid[4] && update_group[4]==7) begin output_index<=0;state<=OUTPUT;end
                    else if((level==0 && update_valid[5] && update_group[5]==7) ||
                            (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3] && predict_group[3]==7)) begin
                        pair_group<=0;level<=level+1'b1;source_bank<=!source_bank;state<=UPDATE;
                    end
                end
                OUTPUT: if(audio_ready) begin
                    if(output_index==15) begin output_index<=0;state<=LOAD;end
                    else output_index<=output_index+1'b1;
                end
                default: state<=LOAD;
            endcase
        end
    end
`ifndef SYNTHESIS
    integer test_pair,test_kind,test_bank,seen;
    reg [5:0] test_address;
    always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n) begin
        if(writing && (state==LOAD || state==OUTPUT)) $fatal(1,"Write outside compute state");
        if(reading) begin
            seen=0;
            for(test_pair=0;test_pair<4;test_pair=test_pair+1)
                for(test_kind=0;test_kind<2;test_kind=test_kind+1) begin
                    test_address=address(level,{pair_group,test_pair[1:0]},test_kind[0]);
                    test_bank=bank_id(test_address);
                    if(seen & (1<<test_bank)) $fatal(1,"Bank read collision");
                    seen=seen | (1<<test_bank);
                end
            if(seen!=255) $fatal(1,"Incomplete bank group");
        end
    end
    initial if(K!=2 || HEADROOM_BITS<0 || HEADROOM_BITS>15) $fatal(1,"Unsupported candidate profile");
`endif
endmodule

===== factored: source and inventories =====
/* Isolated K2 four-pair candidate. N64, two int16 slots and all numeric
 * reductions are unchanged. Each 64x32 working bank is split into eight
 * 8x32 banks; bank={addr[2]^addr[3]^addr[4]^addr[5],addr[1:0]}, row=addr[5:3].
 * This is an invertible physical layout, not a different transform.
 * Level0 boundary pairs use odd=detail+wide_even after the original update.
 * Expected compute: (8+6)+4*(8+10)+(8+5)=99 clocks; not yet qualification.
 */
module ccs_idwpt #(parameter K=2, HEADROOM_BITS=2)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,coeff_valid,
    output wire coeff_ready,
    input wire [31:0] coeff_data,
    output wire audio_valid,
    input wire audio_ready,
    output wire [63:0] audio_data,
    output wire audio_last
);
    localparam LOAD=3'd0, UPDATE=3'd1, UPDATE_DRAIN=3'd2, OUTPUT=3'd5;
    reg [2:0] state,level,pair_group;
    reg [5:0] load_leaf;
    reg source_bank;
    reg [3:0] output_index;
    reg [5:0] update_valid;
    reg [3:0] predict_valid;
    reg [2:0] update_group [0:5],predict_group [0:3];
    reg [3:0] right_tag [0:4];
    reg [3:0] left_tag0,left_boundary1,right_boundary1;
    reg [3:0] left_boundary,right_boundary;
    reg [31:0] previous_detail;
    reg [33:0] pending_even [0:3];
    reg [31:0] pending_detail [0:3];
    reg [3:0] pending_right;
    reg [2:0] pending_group;
    reg pending_valid;
    wire predict_issue=(level!=0 && level!=5) && pending_valid && (pending_right[3] || update_valid[4]);
    wire [33:0] wide_even [0:3];
    wire [31:0] wide_detail [0:3],clipped_even [0:3],clipped_odd [0:3],final_samples [0:3];
    wire [31:0] short_even [0:3],short_odd [0:3];
    wire reading=rst_n && state==UPDATE;
    wire writing=rst_n && ((level==0 && update_valid[5]) || (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3]));
    wire [2:0] write_group=(level==0) ? update_group[5] : predict_group[3];
    // Retimed parity tags travel with the already registered result. No extra
    // data stage: each is computed alongside its final arithmetic register.
    reg early_flip5,normal_flip3;
    wire write_flip=(level==0) ? early_flip5 : normal_flip3;
    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if(update_valid[4] && level==0) early_flip5<=^update_group[4];
        if(predict_valid[2]) normal_flip3<=^predict_group[2];
    end
    wire [31:0] a_read [0:7],b_read [0:7];
    wire [31:0] source0 [0:3],source1 [0:3];

    function [5:0] address;
        input [2:0] current_level;
        input [4:0] pair_number;
        input detail;
        begin
            case(current_level)
                0: address={pair_number,detail};
                1: address={pair_number[4:1],detail,pair_number[0]};
                2: address={pair_number[4:2],detail,pair_number[1:0]};
                3: address={pair_number[4:3],detail,pair_number[2:0]};
                4: address={pair_number[4],detail,pair_number[3:0]};
                default: address={detail,pair_number};
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [2:0] bank_id;
        input [5:0] location;
        begin bank_id={^location[5:2],location[1:0]}; end
    endfunction
    reg [2:0] smooth_row;
    always @* begin
        case(level)
            3: smooth_row={pair_group[2:1],1'b0};
            4: smooth_row={pair_group[2],1'b0,pair_group[1]};
            5: smooth_row={1'b0,pair_group[2:1]};
            default: smooth_row=pair_group;
        endcase
    end
    genvar bank;
    generate for(bank=0;bank<8;bank=bank+1) begin: storage
        reg [31:0] a [0:7],b [0:7];
        reg [31:0] ar,br;
        wire detail_row=bank[2] ^ pair_group[0] ^ (^smooth_row);
        reg [2:0] row;
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                3: row={smooth_row[2:1],detail_row};
                4: row={smooth_row[2],detail_row,smooth_row[0]};
                5: row={detail_row,smooth_row[1:0]};
                default: row=pair_group;
            endcase
        end
        wire load=rst_n && state==LOAD && coeff_valid && bank_id(load_leaf)==bank;
        wire [1:0] pair_select={bank[2] ^ write_flip,bank[1]};
        wire [31:0] even_data=(level==0) ? short_even[pair_select] : clipped_even[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] odd_data=(level==0) ? short_odd[pair_select] : clipped_odd[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] data=bank[0] ? odd_data : even_data;
        always @(posedge clk) begin
            if(load || (writing && source_bank)) a[load ? load_leaf[5:3] : write_group] <= load ? coeff_data : data;
            if(writing && !source_bank) b[write_group] <= data;
            if(reading && !source_bank) ar <= a[row];
            if(reading && source_bank) br <= b[row];
        end
        assign a_read[bank]=ar;
        assign b_read[bank]=br;
    end endgenerate

    // Factor the read permutation into a shared bank-role/parity selection
    // followed by fixed pair wiring for the three low-level permutations.
    wire [31:0] ordered_read [0:7];
    genvar port_index;
    generate for(port_index=0;port_index<8;port_index=port_index+1) begin: read_order
        wire [2:0] selected_bank={port_index[2] ^ (^update_group[0]),port_index[1:0]};
        assign ordered_read[port_index]=source_bank ? b_read[selected_bank] : a_read[selected_bank];
    end endgenerate
    genvar pair;
    generate for(pair=0;pair<4;pair=pair+1) begin: operands
        wire [5:0] smooth=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b0);
        wire [5:0] detail=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b1);
        assign source0[pair]=(level==0) ? ordered_read[2*pair] :
                             (level==1) ? ordered_read[4*(pair/2)+(pair%2)] : ordered_read[pair];
        assign source1[pair]=(level==0) ? ordered_read[2*pair+1] :
                             (level==1) ? ordered_read[4*(pair/2)+(pair%2)+2] : ordered_read[pair+4];
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                0: begin left_boundary[pair]=1;right_boundary[pair]=1;end
                1: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair[0];right_boundary[pair]=pair[0];end
                2: begin left_boundary[pair]=(pair==0);right_boundary[pair]=(pair==3);end
                3: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair_group[0] && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=pair_group[0] && pair==3;end
                4: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group[1:0]==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group[1:0]) && pair==3;end
                default: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group) && pair==3;end
            endcase
        end
    end endgenerate
    function signed [15:0] quarter_rne;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        reg signed [16:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 2;
            if ((value[1:0] == 2'b11) || ((value[1:0] == 2'b10) && value[2]))
                quotient = quotient + 17'sd1;
            quarter_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [16:0] half_rne;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        reg signed [17:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 1;
            if (value[0] && value[1]) quotient = quotient + 18'sd1;
            half_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] clamp18;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        begin
            // An 18-bit value fits int16 exactly when bits 17:15 are a
            // sign extension. Overflow polarity is the original sign bit.
            if (value[17:15] == 3'b000 || value[17:15] == 3'b111)
                clamp18 = value[15:0];
            else clamp18 = value[17] ? 16'sh8000 : 16'sh7fff;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] restore_headroom;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        begin
            // For h>=0, SAT16(SAT16(x)*2^h) == SAT16(x*2^h).
            // Compare before scaling; both thresholds are exact for h<=15.
            // The in-range shift is wiring, with no intermediate truncation.
            if (value > (17'sd32767 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = 16'sd32767;
            else if (value < (-17'sd32768 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = -16'sd32768;
            else restore_headroom = value[15:0] << HEADROOM_BITS;
        end
    endfunction



    genvar q,lane;
    generate for(q=0;q<4;q=q+1) begin: pairs
        for(lane=0;lane<2;lane=lane+1) begin: arithmetic
            reg signed [15:0] smooth1,detail1,left_detail1,smooth2,smooth3,update3;
            reg signed [16:0] detail_sum2,even4,left_even1,right_even1,average3;
            reg signed [17:0] even_sum2,odd4,short_odd5;
            reg signed [15:0] odd_detail1,odd_detail2,odd_detail3,even2,even3,even_for_write4;
            reg signed [15:0] detail2,detail3,detail4,short_even5;
            wire signed [15:0] selected_left1=left_boundary1[q] ? detail1 : left_detail1;
            wire signed [16:0] selected_right1=right_boundary1[q] ? left_even1 : right_even1;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
                if(update_valid[0]) begin
                    smooth1<=source0[q][16*lane +:16];
                    detail1<=source1[q][16*lane +:16];
                    if(q==0) left_detail1<=previous_detail[16*lane +:16];
                    else left_detail1<=source1[q-1][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(update_valid[1]) begin
                    detail_sum2<=$signed({selected_left1[15],selected_left1})+$signed({detail1[15],detail1});
                    smooth2<=smooth1;detail2<=detail1;
                end
                if(update_valid[2]) begin
                    update3<=quarter_rne(detail_sum2);smooth3<=smooth2;detail3<=detail2;
                end
                if(update_valid[3]) begin
                    even4<=$signed({smooth3[15],smooth3})-$signed({update3[15],update3});detail4<=detail3;
                end
                if(update_valid[4] && level==0) begin
                    short_even5<=clamp18({even4[16],even4});
                    short_odd5<=$signed({{2{detail4[15]}},detail4})+$signed({even4[16],even4});
                end
                if(predict_issue) begin
                    left_even1<=pending_even[q][17*lane +:17];
                    if(q==3) right_even1<=wide_even[0][17*lane +:17];
                    else right_even1<=pending_even[q+1][17*lane +:17];
                    odd_detail1<=pending_detail[q][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(predict_valid[0]) begin
                    even_sum2<=$signed({left_even1[16],left_even1})+$signed({selected_right1[16],selected_right1});
                    even2<=clamp18({left_even1[16],left_even1});odd_detail2<=odd_detail1;
                end
                if(predict_valid[1]) begin
                    average3<=half_rne(even_sum2);odd_detail3<=odd_detail2;even3<=even2;
                end
                if(predict_valid[2]) begin
                    odd4<=$signed({{2{odd_detail3[15]}},odd_detail3})+$signed({average3[16],average3});
                    even_for_write4<=even3;
                end
            end
            assign wide_even[q][17*lane +:17]=even4;
            assign wide_detail[q][16*lane +:16]=detail4;
            assign final_samples[q][16*lane +:16]=restore_headroom(even4);
            assign clipped_even[q][16*lane +:16]=even_for_write4;
            assign clipped_odd[q][16*lane +:16]=clamp18(odd4);
            assign short_even[q][16*lane +:16]=short_even5;
            assign short_odd[q][16*lane +:16]=clamp18(short_odd5);
        end
        always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_even[q]<=wide_even[q];pending_detail[q]<=wide_detail[q];
        end
    end endgenerate

    reg [63:0] output_lo [0:7],output_hi [0:7];
    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if(rst_n && update_valid[0]) begin previous_detail<=source1[3];left_boundary1<=left_tag0;end
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_right<=right_tag[4];pending_group<=update_group[4];
        end
        if(rst_n && predict_issue) right_boundary1<=pending_right;
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level==5) begin
            output_lo[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[1],final_samples[0]};
            output_hi[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[3],final_samples[2]};
        end
    end
    assign coeff_ready=rst_n && state==LOAD;
    assign audio_valid=rst_n && state==OUTPUT;
    assign audio_last=audio_valid && output_index==15;
    assign audio_data=output_index[0] ? output_hi[output_index[3:1]] : output_lo[output_index[3:1]];
    integer stage;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            state<=LOAD;level<=0;pair_group<=0;load_leaf<=0;source_bank<=0;output_index<=0;
            update_valid<=0;predict_valid<=0;pending_valid<=0;
        end else begin
            update_valid<={update_valid[4:0],state==UPDATE};
            predict_valid<={predict_valid[2:0],predict_issue};
            update_group[0]<=pair_group;predict_group[0]<=pending_group;
            left_tag0<=left_boundary;right_tag[0]<=right_boundary;
            for(stage=1;stage<6;stage=stage+1) update_group[stage]<=update_group[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<5;stage=stage+1) right_tag[stage]<=right_tag[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<4;stage=stage+1) predict_group[stage]<=predict_group[stage-1];
            if(update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) pending_valid<=1;
            else if(predict_issue) pending_valid<=0;
            case(state)
                LOAD: if(coeff_valid) begin
                    if(load_leaf==63) begin load_leaf<=0;pair_group<=0;level<=0;source_bank<=0;state<=UPDATE;end
                    else load_leaf<=load_leaf+1'b1;
                end
                UPDATE: if(pair_group==7) state<=UPDATE_DRAIN;
                        else pair_group<=pair_group+1'b1;
                UPDATE_DRAIN: begin
                    if(level==5 && update_valid[4] && update_group[4]==7) begin output_index<=0;state<=OUTPUT;end
                    else if((level==0 && update_valid[5] && update_group[5]==7) ||
                            (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3] && predict_group[3]==7)) begin
                        pair_group<=0;level<=level+1'b1;source_bank<=!source_bank;state<=UPDATE;
                    end
                end
                OUTPUT: if(audio_ready) begin
                    if(output_index==15) begin output_index<=0;state<=LOAD;end
                    else output_index<=output_index+1'b1;
                end
                default: state<=LOAD;
            endcase
        end
    end
`ifndef SYNTHESIS
    integer test_pair,test_kind,test_bank,seen;
    reg [5:0] test_address;
    always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n) begin
        if(writing && write_flip!=(^write_group)) $fatal(1,"Write parity tag does not match result");
        if(writing && (state==LOAD || state==OUTPUT)) $fatal(1,"Write outside compute state");
        if(reading) begin
            seen=0;
            for(test_pair=0;test_pair<4;test_pair=test_pair+1)
                for(test_kind=0;test_kind<2;test_kind=test_kind+1) begin
                    test_address=address(level,{pair_group,test_pair[1:0]},test_kind[0]);
                    test_bank=bank_id(test_address);
                    if(seen & (1<<test_bank)) $fatal(1,"Bank read collision");
                    seen=seen | (1<<test_bank);
                end
            if(seen!=255) $fatal(1,"Incomplete bank group");
        end
    end
    initial if(K!=2 || HEADROOM_BITS<0 || HEADROOM_BITS>15) $fatal(1,"Unsupported candidate profile");
`endif
endmodule

===== shared: source and inventories =====
/* Isolated K2 four-pair candidate. N64, two int16 slots and all numeric
 * reductions are unchanged. Each 64x32 working bank is split into eight
 * 8x32 banks; bank={addr[2]^addr[3]^addr[4]^addr[5],addr[1:0]}, row=addr[5:3].
 * This is an invertible physical layout, not a different transform.
 * Level0 boundary pairs use odd=detail+wide_even after the original update.
 * Expected compute: (8+6)+4*(8+10)+(8+5)=99 clocks; not yet qualification.
 */
module ccs_idwpt #(parameter K=2, HEADROOM_BITS=2)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,coeff_valid,
    output wire coeff_ready,
    input wire [31:0] coeff_data,
    output wire audio_valid,
    input wire audio_ready,
    output wire [63:0] audio_data,
    output wire audio_last
);
    localparam LOAD=3'd0, UPDATE=3'd1, UPDATE_DRAIN=3'd2, OUTPUT=3'd5;
    reg [2:0] state,level,pair_group;
    reg [5:0] load_leaf;
    reg source_bank;
    reg [3:0] output_index;
    reg [5:0] update_valid;
    reg [3:0] predict_valid;
    reg [2:0] update_group [0:5],predict_group [0:3];
    reg [3:0] right_tag [0:4];
    reg [3:0] left_tag0,left_boundary1,right_boundary1;
    reg [3:0] left_boundary,right_boundary;
    reg [31:0] previous_detail;
    reg [33:0] pending_even [0:3];
    reg [31:0] pending_detail [0:3];
    reg [3:0] pending_right;
    reg [2:0] pending_group;
    reg pending_valid;
    wire predict_issue=(level!=0 && level!=5) && pending_valid && (pending_right[3] || update_valid[4]);
    wire [33:0] wide_even [0:3];
    wire [31:0] wide_detail [0:3],clipped_even [0:3],clipped_odd [0:3],final_samples [0:3];
    wire reading=rst_n && state==UPDATE;
    wire writing=rst_n && ((level==0 && update_valid[5]) || (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3]));
    wire [2:0] write_group=(level==0) ? update_group[5] : predict_group[3];
    // Retimed parity tags travel with the already registered result. No extra
    // data stage: each is computed alongside its final arithmetic register.
    reg write_flip;
    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if(level==0) begin
            if(update_valid[4]) write_flip<=^update_group[4];
        end else if(predict_valid[2]) write_flip<=^predict_group[2];
    end
    wire [31:0] a_read [0:7],b_read [0:7];
    wire [31:0] source0 [0:3],source1 [0:3];

    function [5:0] address;
        input [2:0] current_level;
        input [4:0] pair_number;
        input detail;
        begin
            case(current_level)
                0: address={pair_number,detail};
                1: address={pair_number[4:1],detail,pair_number[0]};
                2: address={pair_number[4:2],detail,pair_number[1:0]};
                3: address={pair_number[4:3],detail,pair_number[2:0]};
                4: address={pair_number[4],detail,pair_number[3:0]};
                default: address={detail,pair_number};
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [2:0] bank_id;
        input [5:0] location;
        begin bank_id={^location[5:2],location[1:0]}; end
    endfunction
    reg [2:0] smooth_row;
    always @* begin
        case(level)
            3: smooth_row={pair_group[2:1],1'b0};
            4: smooth_row={pair_group[2],1'b0,pair_group[1]};
            5: smooth_row={1'b0,pair_group[2:1]};
            default: smooth_row=pair_group;
        endcase
    end
    genvar bank;
    generate for(bank=0;bank<8;bank=bank+1) begin: storage
        reg [31:0] a [0:7],b [0:7];
        reg [31:0] ar,br;
        wire detail_row=bank[2] ^ pair_group[0] ^ (^smooth_row);
        reg [2:0] row;
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                3: row={smooth_row[2:1],detail_row};
                4: row={smooth_row[2],detail_row,smooth_row[0]};
                5: row={detail_row,smooth_row[1:0]};
                default: row=pair_group;
            endcase
        end
        wire load=rst_n && state==LOAD && coeff_valid && bank_id(load_leaf)==bank;
        wire [1:0] pair_select={bank[2] ^ write_flip,bank[1]};
        wire [31:0] even_data=clipped_even[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] odd_data=clipped_odd[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] data=bank[0] ? odd_data : even_data;
        always @(posedge clk) begin
            if(load || (writing && source_bank)) a[load ? load_leaf[5:3] : write_group] <= load ? coeff_data : data;
            if(writing && !source_bank) b[write_group] <= data;
            if(reading && !source_bank) ar <= a[row];
            if(reading && source_bank) br <= b[row];
        end
        assign a_read[bank]=ar;
        assign b_read[bank]=br;
    end endgenerate

    // Factor the read permutation into a shared bank-role/parity selection
    // followed by fixed pair wiring for the three low-level permutations.
    wire [31:0] ordered_read [0:7];
    genvar port_index;
    generate for(port_index=0;port_index<8;port_index=port_index+1) begin: read_order
        wire [2:0] selected_bank={port_index[2] ^ (^update_group[0]),port_index[1:0]};
        assign ordered_read[port_index]=source_bank ? b_read[selected_bank] : a_read[selected_bank];
    end endgenerate
    genvar pair;
    generate for(pair=0;pair<4;pair=pair+1) begin: operands
        wire [5:0] smooth=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b0);
        wire [5:0] detail=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b1);
        assign source0[pair]=(level==0) ? ordered_read[2*pair] :
                             (level==1) ? ordered_read[4*(pair/2)+(pair%2)] : ordered_read[pair];
        assign source1[pair]=(level==0) ? ordered_read[2*pair+1] :
                             (level==1) ? ordered_read[4*(pair/2)+(pair%2)+2] : ordered_read[pair+4];
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                0: begin left_boundary[pair]=1;right_boundary[pair]=1;end
                1: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair[0];right_boundary[pair]=pair[0];end
                2: begin left_boundary[pair]=(pair==0);right_boundary[pair]=(pair==3);end
                3: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair_group[0] && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=pair_group[0] && pair==3;end
                4: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group[1:0]==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group[1:0]) && pair==3;end
                default: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group) && pair==3;end
            endcase
        end
    end endgenerate
    function signed [15:0] quarter_rne;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        reg signed [16:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 2;
            if ((value[1:0] == 2'b11) || ((value[1:0] == 2'b10) && value[2]))
                quotient = quotient + 17'sd1;
            quarter_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [16:0] half_rne;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        reg signed [17:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 1;
            if (value[0] && value[1]) quotient = quotient + 18'sd1;
            half_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] clamp18;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        begin
            // An 18-bit value fits int16 exactly when bits 17:15 are a
            // sign extension. Overflow polarity is the original sign bit.
            if (value[17:15] == 3'b000 || value[17:15] == 3'b111)
                clamp18 = value[15:0];
            else clamp18 = value[17] ? 16'sh8000 : 16'sh7fff;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] restore_headroom;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        begin
            // For h>=0, SAT16(SAT16(x)*2^h) == SAT16(x*2^h).
            // Compare before scaling; both thresholds are exact for h<=15.
            // The in-range shift is wiring, with no intermediate truncation.
            if (value > (17'sd32767 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = 16'sd32767;
            else if (value < (-17'sd32768 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = -16'sd32768;
            else restore_headroom = value[15:0] << HEADROOM_BITS;
        end
    endfunction



    genvar q,lane;
    generate for(q=0;q<4;q=q+1) begin: pairs
        for(lane=0;lane<2;lane=lane+1) begin: arithmetic
            reg signed [15:0] smooth1,detail1,left_detail1,smooth2,smooth3,update3;
            reg signed [16:0] detail_sum2,even4,left_even1,right_even1,average3;
            reg signed [17:0] even_sum2,odd4;
            reg signed [15:0] odd_detail1,odd_detail2,odd_detail3,even2,even3,even_for_write4;
            reg signed [15:0] detail2,detail3,detail4;
            wire signed [15:0] selected_left1=left_boundary1[q] ? detail1 : left_detail1;
            wire signed [16:0] selected_right1=right_boundary1[q] ? left_even1 : right_even1;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
                if(update_valid[0]) begin
                    smooth1<=source0[q][16*lane +:16];
                    detail1<=source1[q][16*lane +:16];
                    if(q==0) left_detail1<=previous_detail[16*lane +:16];
                    else left_detail1<=source1[q-1][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(update_valid[1]) begin
                    detail_sum2<=$signed({selected_left1[15],selected_left1})+$signed({detail1[15],detail1});
                    smooth2<=smooth1;detail2<=detail1;
                end
                if(update_valid[2]) begin
                    update3<=quarter_rne(detail_sum2);smooth3<=smooth2;detail3<=detail2;
                end
                if(update_valid[3]) begin
                    even4<=$signed({smooth3[15],smooth3})-$signed({update3[15],update3});detail4<=detail3;
                end
                if(update_valid[4] && level==0) begin
                    even_for_write4<=clamp18({even4[16],even4});
                    odd4<=$signed({{2{detail4[15]}},detail4})+$signed({even4[16],even4});
                end
                if(predict_issue) begin
                    left_even1<=pending_even[q][17*lane +:17];
                    if(q==3) right_even1<=wide_even[0][17*lane +:17];
                    else right_even1<=pending_even[q+1][17*lane +:17];
                    odd_detail1<=pending_detail[q][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(predict_valid[0]) begin
                    even_sum2<=$signed({left_even1[16],left_even1})+$signed({selected_right1[16],selected_right1});
                    even2<=clamp18({left_even1[16],left_even1});odd_detail2<=odd_detail1;
                end
                if(predict_valid[1]) begin
                    average3<=half_rne(even_sum2);odd_detail3<=odd_detail2;even3<=even2;
                end
                if(predict_valid[2] && level!=0) begin
                    odd4<=$signed({{2{odd_detail3[15]}},odd_detail3})+$signed({average3[16],average3});
                    even_for_write4<=even3;
                end
            end
            assign wide_even[q][17*lane +:17]=even4;
            assign wide_detail[q][16*lane +:16]=detail4;
            assign final_samples[q][16*lane +:16]=restore_headroom(even4);
            assign clipped_even[q][16*lane +:16]=even_for_write4;
            assign clipped_odd[q][16*lane +:16]=clamp18(odd4);
        end
        always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_even[q]<=wide_even[q];pending_detail[q]<=wide_detail[q];
        end
    end endgenerate

    reg [63:0] output_lo [0:7],output_hi [0:7];
    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if(rst_n && update_valid[0]) begin previous_detail<=source1[3];left_boundary1<=left_tag0;end
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_right<=right_tag[4];pending_group<=update_group[4];
        end
        if(rst_n && predict_issue) right_boundary1<=pending_right;
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level==5) begin
            output_lo[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[1],final_samples[0]};
            output_hi[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[3],final_samples[2]};
        end
    end
    assign coeff_ready=rst_n && state==LOAD;
    assign audio_valid=rst_n && state==OUTPUT;
    assign audio_last=audio_valid && output_index==15;
    assign audio_data=output_index[0] ? output_hi[output_index[3:1]] : output_lo[output_index[3:1]];
    integer stage;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            state<=LOAD;level<=0;pair_group<=0;load_leaf<=0;source_bank<=0;output_index<=0;
            update_valid<=0;predict_valid<=0;pending_valid<=0;
        end else begin
            update_valid<={update_valid[4:0],state==UPDATE};
            predict_valid<={predict_valid[2:0],predict_issue};
            update_group[0]<=pair_group;predict_group[0]<=pending_group;
            left_tag0<=left_boundary;right_tag[0]<=right_boundary;
            for(stage=1;stage<6;stage=stage+1) update_group[stage]<=update_group[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<5;stage=stage+1) right_tag[stage]<=right_tag[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<4;stage=stage+1) predict_group[stage]<=predict_group[stage-1];
            if(update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) pending_valid<=1;
            else if(predict_issue) pending_valid<=0;
            case(state)
                LOAD: if(coeff_valid) begin
                    if(load_leaf==63) begin load_leaf<=0;pair_group<=0;level<=0;source_bank<=0;state<=UPDATE;end
                    else load_leaf<=load_leaf+1'b1;
                end
                UPDATE: if(pair_group==7) state<=UPDATE_DRAIN;
                        else pair_group<=pair_group+1'b1;
                UPDATE_DRAIN: begin
                    if(level==5 && update_valid[4] && update_group[4]==7) begin output_index<=0;state<=OUTPUT;end
                    else if((level==0 && update_valid[5] && update_group[5]==7) ||
                            (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3] && predict_group[3]==7)) begin
                        pair_group<=0;level<=level+1'b1;source_bank<=!source_bank;state<=UPDATE;
                    end
                end
                OUTPUT: if(audio_ready) begin
                    if(output_index==15) begin output_index<=0;state<=LOAD;end
                    else output_index<=output_index+1'b1;
                end
                default: state<=LOAD;
            endcase
        end
    end
`ifndef SYNTHESIS
    integer test_pair,test_kind,test_bank,seen;
    reg [5:0] test_address;
    always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n) begin
        if(writing && write_flip!=(^write_group)) $fatal(1,"Write parity tag does not match result");
        if(writing && (state==LOAD || state==OUTPUT)) $fatal(1,"Write outside compute state");
        if(reading) begin
            seen=0;
            for(test_pair=0;test_pair<4;test_pair=test_pair+1)
                for(test_kind=0;test_kind<2;test_kind=test_kind+1) begin
                    test_address=address(level,{pair_group,test_pair[1:0]},test_kind[0]);
                    test_bank=bank_id(test_address);
                    if(seen & (1<<test_bank)) $fatal(1,"Bank read collision");
                    seen=seen | (1<<test_bank);
                end
            if(seen!=255) $fatal(1,"Incomplete bank group");
        end
    end
    initial if(K!=2 || HEADROOM_BITS<0 || HEADROOM_BITS>15) $fatal(1,"Unsupported candidate profile");
`endif
endmodule

===== flag: source and inventories =====
/* Isolated K2 four-pair candidate. N64, two int16 slots and all numeric
 * reductions are unchanged. Each 64x32 working bank is split into eight
 * 8x32 banks; bank={addr[2]^addr[3]^addr[4]^addr[5],addr[1:0]}, row=addr[5:3].
 * This is an invertible physical layout, not a different transform.
 * Level0 boundary pairs use odd=detail+wide_even after the original update.
 * Expected compute: (8+6)+4*(8+10)+(8+5)=99 clocks; not yet qualification.
 */
module ccs_idwpt #(parameter K=2, HEADROOM_BITS=2)(
    input wire clk,rst_n,coeff_valid,
    output wire coeff_ready,
    input wire [31:0] coeff_data,
    output wire audio_valid,
    input wire audio_ready,
    output wire [63:0] audio_data,
    output wire audio_last
);
    localparam LOAD=3'd0, UPDATE=3'd1, UPDATE_DRAIN=3'd2, OUTPUT=3'd5;
    reg [2:0] state,level,pair_group;
    reg [5:0] load_leaf;
    reg source_bank;
    reg first_level; // Registered level==0 decode for the wide writeback selects.
    reg [3:0] output_index;
    reg [5:0] update_valid;
    reg [3:0] predict_valid;
    reg [2:0] update_group [0:5],predict_group [0:3];
    reg [3:0] right_tag [0:4];
    reg [3:0] left_tag0,left_boundary1,right_boundary1;
    reg [3:0] left_boundary,right_boundary;
    reg [31:0] previous_detail;
    reg [33:0] pending_even [0:3];
    reg [31:0] pending_detail [0:3];
    reg [3:0] pending_right;
    reg [2:0] pending_group;
    reg pending_valid;
    wire predict_issue=(level!=0 && level!=5) && pending_valid && (pending_right[3] || update_valid[4]);
    wire [33:0] wide_even [0:3];
    wire [31:0] wide_detail [0:3],clipped_even [0:3],clipped_odd [0:3],final_samples [0:3];
    wire [31:0] short_even [0:3],short_odd [0:3];
    wire reading=rst_n && state==UPDATE;
    wire writing=rst_n && ((level==0 && update_valid[5]) || (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3]));
    wire [2:0] write_group=first_level ? update_group[5] : predict_group[3];
    // Retimed parity tags travel with the already registered result. No extra
    // data stage: each is computed alongside its final arithmetic register.
    reg early_flip5,normal_flip3;
    wire write_flip=first_level ? early_flip5 : normal_flip3;
    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if(update_valid[4] && level==0) early_flip5<=^update_group[4];
        if(predict_valid[2]) normal_flip3<=^predict_group[2];
    end
    wire [31:0] a_read [0:7],b_read [0:7];
    wire [31:0] source0 [0:3],source1 [0:3];

    function [5:0] address;
        input [2:0] current_level;
        input [4:0] pair_number;
        input detail;
        begin
            case(current_level)
                0: address={pair_number,detail};
                1: address={pair_number[4:1],detail,pair_number[0]};
                2: address={pair_number[4:2],detail,pair_number[1:0]};
                3: address={pair_number[4:3],detail,pair_number[2:0]};
                4: address={pair_number[4],detail,pair_number[3:0]};
                default: address={detail,pair_number};
            endcase
        end
    endfunction
    function [2:0] bank_id;
        input [5:0] location;
        begin bank_id={^location[5:2],location[1:0]}; end
    endfunction
    reg [2:0] smooth_row;
    always @* begin
        case(level)
            3: smooth_row={pair_group[2:1],1'b0};
            4: smooth_row={pair_group[2],1'b0,pair_group[1]};
            5: smooth_row={1'b0,pair_group[2:1]};
            default: smooth_row=pair_group;
        endcase
    end
    genvar bank;
    generate for(bank=0;bank<8;bank=bank+1) begin: storage
        reg [31:0] a [0:7],b [0:7];
        reg [31:0] ar,br;
        wire detail_row=bank[2] ^ pair_group[0] ^ (^smooth_row);
        reg [2:0] row;
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                3: row={smooth_row[2:1],detail_row};
                4: row={smooth_row[2],detail_row,smooth_row[0]};
                5: row={detail_row,smooth_row[1:0]};
                default: row=pair_group;
            endcase
        end
        wire load=rst_n && state==LOAD && coeff_valid && bank_id(load_leaf)==bank;
        wire [1:0] pair_select={bank[2] ^ write_flip,bank[1]};
        wire [31:0] even_data=first_level ? short_even[pair_select] : clipped_even[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] odd_data=first_level ? short_odd[pair_select] : clipped_odd[pair_select];
        wire [31:0] data=bank[0] ? odd_data : even_data;
        always @(posedge clk) begin
            if(load || (writing && source_bank)) a[load ? load_leaf[5:3] : write_group] <= load ? coeff_data : data;
            if(writing && !source_bank) b[write_group] <= data;
            if(reading && !source_bank) ar <= a[row];
            if(reading && source_bank) br <= b[row];
        end
        assign a_read[bank]=ar;
        assign b_read[bank]=br;
    end endgenerate

    // Factor the read permutation into a shared bank-role/parity selection
    // followed by fixed pair wiring for the three low-level permutations.
    wire [31:0] ordered_read [0:7];
    genvar port_index;
    generate for(port_index=0;port_index<8;port_index=port_index+1) begin: read_order
        wire [2:0] selected_bank={port_index[2] ^ (^update_group[0]),port_index[1:0]};
        assign ordered_read[port_index]=source_bank ? b_read[selected_bank] : a_read[selected_bank];
    end endgenerate
    genvar pair;
    generate for(pair=0;pair<4;pair=pair+1) begin: operands
        wire [5:0] smooth=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b0);
        wire [5:0] detail=address(level,{update_group[0],pair[1:0]},1'b1);
        assign source0[pair]=(level==0) ? ordered_read[2*pair] :
                             (level==1) ? ordered_read[4*(pair/2)+(pair%2)] : ordered_read[pair];
        assign source1[pair]=(level==0) ? ordered_read[2*pair+1] :
                             (level==1) ? ordered_read[4*(pair/2)+(pair%2)+2] : ordered_read[pair+4];
        always @* begin
            case(level)
                0: begin left_boundary[pair]=1;right_boundary[pair]=1;end
                1: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair[0];right_boundary[pair]=pair[0];end
                2: begin left_boundary[pair]=(pair==0);right_boundary[pair]=(pair==3);end
                3: begin left_boundary[pair]=!pair_group[0] && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=pair_group[0] && pair==3;end
                4: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group[1:0]==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group[1:0]) && pair==3;end
                default: begin left_boundary[pair]=pair_group==0 && pair==0;right_boundary[pair]=(&pair_group) && pair==3;end
            endcase
        end
    end endgenerate
    function signed [15:0] quarter_rne;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        reg signed [16:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 2;
            if ((value[1:0] == 2'b11) || ((value[1:0] == 2'b10) && value[2]))
                quotient = quotient + 17'sd1;
            quarter_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [16:0] half_rne;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        reg signed [17:0] quotient;
        begin
            quotient = value >>> 1;
            if (value[0] && value[1]) quotient = quotient + 18'sd1;
            half_rne = quotient;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] clamp18;
        input signed [17:0] value;
        begin
            // An 18-bit value fits int16 exactly when bits 17:15 are a
            // sign extension. Overflow polarity is the original sign bit.
            if (value[17:15] == 3'b000 || value[17:15] == 3'b111)
                clamp18 = value[15:0];
            else clamp18 = value[17] ? 16'sh8000 : 16'sh7fff;
        end
    endfunction

    function signed [15:0] restore_headroom;
        input signed [16:0] value;
        begin
            // For h>=0, SAT16(SAT16(x)*2^h) == SAT16(x*2^h).
            // Compare before scaling; both thresholds are exact for h<=15.
            // The in-range shift is wiring, with no intermediate truncation.
            if (value > (17'sd32767 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = 16'sd32767;
            else if (value < (-17'sd32768 >>> HEADROOM_BITS)) restore_headroom = -16'sd32768;
            else restore_headroom = value[15:0] << HEADROOM_BITS;
        end
    endfunction



    genvar q,lane;
    generate for(q=0;q<4;q=q+1) begin: pairs
        for(lane=0;lane<2;lane=lane+1) begin: arithmetic
            reg signed [15:0] smooth1,detail1,left_detail1,smooth2,smooth3,update3;
            reg signed [16:0] detail_sum2,even4,left_even1,right_even1,average3;
            reg signed [17:0] even_sum2,odd4,short_odd5;
            reg signed [15:0] odd_detail1,odd_detail2,odd_detail3,even2,even3,even_for_write4;
            reg signed [15:0] detail2,detail3,detail4,short_even5;
            wire signed [15:0] selected_left1=left_boundary1[q] ? detail1 : left_detail1;
            wire signed [16:0] selected_right1=right_boundary1[q] ? left_even1 : right_even1;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
                if(update_valid[0]) begin
                    smooth1<=source0[q][16*lane +:16];
                    detail1<=source1[q][16*lane +:16];
                    if(q==0) left_detail1<=previous_detail[16*lane +:16];
                    else left_detail1<=source1[q-1][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(update_valid[1]) begin
                    detail_sum2<=$signed({selected_left1[15],selected_left1})+$signed({detail1[15],detail1});
                    smooth2<=smooth1;detail2<=detail1;
                end
                if(update_valid[2]) begin
                    update3<=quarter_rne(detail_sum2);smooth3<=smooth2;detail3<=detail2;
                end
                if(update_valid[3]) begin
                    even4<=$signed({smooth3[15],smooth3})-$signed({update3[15],update3});detail4<=detail3;
                end
                if(update_valid[4] && level==0) begin
                    short_even5<=clamp18({even4[16],even4});
                    short_odd5<=$signed({{2{detail4[15]}},detail4})+$signed({even4[16],even4});
                end
                if(predict_issue) begin
                    left_even1<=pending_even[q][17*lane +:17];
                    if(q==3) right_even1<=wide_even[0][17*lane +:17];
                    else right_even1<=pending_even[q+1][17*lane +:17];
                    odd_detail1<=pending_detail[q][16*lane +:16];
                end
                if(predict_valid[0]) begin
                    even_sum2<=$signed({left_even1[16],left_even1})+$signed({selected_right1[16],selected_right1});
                    even2<=clamp18({left_even1[16],left_even1});odd_detail2<=odd_detail1;
                end
                if(predict_valid[1]) begin
                    average3<=half_rne(even_sum2);odd_detail3<=odd_detail2;even3<=even2;
                end
                if(predict_valid[2]) begin
                    odd4<=$signed({{2{odd_detail3[15]}},odd_detail3})+$signed({average3[16],average3});
                    even_for_write4<=even3;
                end
            end
            assign wide_even[q][17*lane +:17]=even4;
            assign wide_detail[q][16*lane +:16]=detail4;
            assign final_samples[q][16*lane +:16]=restore_headroom(even4);
            assign clipped_even[q][16*lane +:16]=even_for_write4;
            assign clipped_odd[q][16*lane +:16]=clamp18(odd4);
            assign short_even[q][16*lane +:16]=short_even5;
            assign short_odd[q][16*lane +:16]=clamp18(short_odd5);
        end
        always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_even[q]<=wide_even[q];pending_detail[q]<=wide_detail[q];
        end
    end endgenerate

    reg [63:0] output_lo [0:7],output_hi [0:7];
    always @(posedge clk) begin
        if(rst_n && update_valid[0]) begin previous_detail<=source1[3];left_boundary1<=left_tag0;end
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) begin
            pending_right<=right_tag[4];pending_group<=update_group[4];
        end
        if(rst_n && predict_issue) right_boundary1<=pending_right;
        if(rst_n && update_valid[4] && level==5) begin
            output_lo[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[1],final_samples[0]};
            output_hi[update_group[4]]<={final_samples[3],final_samples[2]};
        end
    end
    assign coeff_ready=rst_n && state==LOAD;
    assign audio_valid=rst_n && state==OUTPUT;
    assign audio_last=audio_valid && output_index==15;
    assign audio_data=output_index[0] ? output_hi[output_index[3:1]] : output_lo[output_index[3:1]];
    integer stage;
    always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
        if(!rst_n) begin
            state<=LOAD;level<=0;first_level<=1;pair_group<=0;load_leaf<=0;source_bank<=0;output_index<=0;
            update_valid<=0;predict_valid<=0;pending_valid<=0;
        end else begin
            update_valid<={update_valid[4:0],state==UPDATE};
            predict_valid<={predict_valid[2:0],predict_issue};
            update_group[0]<=pair_group;predict_group[0]<=pending_group;
            left_tag0<=left_boundary;right_tag[0]<=right_boundary;
            for(stage=1;stage<6;stage=stage+1) update_group[stage]<=update_group[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<5;stage=stage+1) right_tag[stage]<=right_tag[stage-1];
            for(stage=1;stage<4;stage=stage+1) predict_group[stage]<=predict_group[stage-1];
            if(update_valid[4] && level!=0 && level!=5) pending_valid<=1;
            else if(predict_issue) pending_valid<=0;
            case(state)
                LOAD: if(coeff_valid) begin
                    if(load_leaf==63) begin load_leaf<=0;pair_group<=0;level<=0;first_level<=1;source_bank<=0;state<=UPDATE;end
                    else load_leaf<=load_leaf+1'b1;
                end
                UPDATE: if(pair_group==7) state<=UPDATE_DRAIN;
                        else pair_group<=pair_group+1'b1;
                UPDATE_DRAIN: begin
                    if(level==5 && update_valid[4] && update_group[4]==7) begin output_index<=0;state<=OUTPUT;end
                    else if((level==0 && update_valid[5] && update_group[5]==7) ||
                            (level!=0 && level!=5 && predict_valid[3] && predict_group[3]==7)) begin
                        pair_group<=0;level<=level+1'b1;first_level<=0;source_bank<=!source_bank;state<=UPDATE;
                    end
                end
                OUTPUT: if(audio_ready) begin
                    if(output_index==15) begin output_index<=0;state<=LOAD;end
                    else output_index<=output_index+1'b1;
                end
                default: state<=LOAD;
            endcase
        end
    end
`ifndef SYNTHESIS
    integer test_pair,test_kind,test_bank,seen;
    reg [5:0] test_address;
    always @(posedge clk) if(rst_n) begin
        if(first_level!=(level==0)) $fatal(1,"Registered level decode mismatch");
        if(writing && write_flip!=(^write_group)) $fatal(1,"Write parity tag does not match result");
        if(writing && (state==LOAD || state==OUTPUT)) $fatal(1,"Write outside compute state");
        if(reading) begin
            seen=0;
            for(test_pair=0;test_pair<4;test_pair=test_pair+1)
                for(test_kind=0;test_kind<2;test_kind=test_kind+1) begin
                    test_address=address(level,{pair_group,test_pair[1:0]},test_kind[0]);
                    test_bank=bank_id(test_address);
                    if(seen & (1<<test_bank)) $fatal(1,"Bank read collision");
                    seen=seen | (1<<test_bank);
                end
            if(seen!=255) $fatal(1,"Incomplete bank group");
        end
    end
    initial if(K!=2 || HEADROOM_BITS<0 || HEADROOM_BITS>15) $fatal(1,"Unsupported candidate profile");
`endif
endmodule
